{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01HLkcgV6RvXyYyRfUFtx477","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"#!/usr/bin/env python\\\\n\\\\n# Copyright 2025 HuggingFace Inc. team. All rights reserved.\\\\n#\\\\n# Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \\\\\\\"License\\\\\\\");\\\\n# you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\\\\n# You may obtain a copy of the License at\\\\n#\\\\n#     http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\\\\n#\\\\n# Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\\\\n# distributed under the License is distributed on an \\\\\\\"AS IS\\\\\\\" BASIS,\\\\n# WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\\\\n# See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\\\\n# limitations under the License.\\\\n\\\\nfrom typing import Any\\\\n\\\\nimport torch\\\\n\\\\nfrom lerobot.configs.types import PipelineFeatureType, PolicyFeature\\\\nfrom lerobot.policies.smolvla.configuration_smolvla import SmolVLAConfig\\\\nfrom lerobot.processor import (\\\\n    AddBatchDimensionProcessorStep,\\\\n    ComplementaryDataProcessorStep,\\\\n    DeviceProcessorStep,\\\\n    NormalizerProcessorStep,\\\\n    PolicyAction,\\\\n    PolicyProcessorPipeline,\\\\n    ProcessorStepRegistry,\\\\n    RenameObservationsProcessorStep,\\\\n    TokenizerProcessorStep,\\\\n    UnnormalizerProcessorStep,\\\\n)\\\\nfrom lerobot.processor.converters import policy_action_to_transition, transition_to_policy_action\\\\nfrom lerobot.utils.constants import POLICY_POSTPROCESSOR_DEFAULT_NAME, POLICY_PREPROCESSOR_DEFAULT_NAME\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef make_smolvla_pre_post_processors(\\\\n    config: SmolVLAConfig,\\\\n    dataset_stats: dict[str, dict[str, torch.Tensor]] | None = None,\\\\n) -> tuple[\\\\n    PolicyProcessorPipeline[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]],\\\\n    PolicyProcessorPipeline[PolicyAction, PolicyAction],\\\\n]:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    Constructs pre-processor and post-processor pipelines for the SmolVLA policy.\\\\n\\\\n    The pre-processing pipeline prepares input data for the model by:\\\\n    1.  Renaming features to match pretrained configurations.\\\\n    2.  Normalizing input and output features based on dataset statistics.\\\\n    3.  Adding a batch dimension.\\\\n    4.  Ensuring the language task description ends with a newline character.\\\\n    5.  Tokenizing the language task description.\\\\n    6.  Moving all data to the specified device.\\\\n\\\\n    The post-processing pipeline handles the model's output by:\\\\n    1.  Moving data to the CPU.\\\\n    2.  Unnormalizing the output actions to their original scale.\\\\n\\\\n    Args:\\\\n        config: The configuration object for the SmolVLA policy.\\\\n        dataset_stats: A dictionary of statistics for normalization.\\\\n\\\\n    Returns:\\\\n        A tuple containing the configured pre-processor and post-processor pipelines.\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    input_steps = [\\\\n        RenameObservationsProcessorStep(rename_map={}),  # To mimic the same processor as pretrained one\\\\n        AddBatchDimensionProcessorStep(),\\\\n        SmolVLANewLineProcessor(),\\\\n        TokenizerProcessorStep(\\\\n            tokenizer_name=config.vlm_model_name,\\\\n            padding=config.pad_language_to,\\\\n            padding_side=\\\\\\\"right\\\\\\\",\\\\n            max_length=config.tokenizer_max_length,\\\\n        ),\\\\n        DeviceProcessorStep(device=config.device),\\\\n        NormalizerProcessorStep(\\\\n            features={**config.input_features, **config.output_features},\\\\n            norm_map=config.normalization_mapping,\\\\n            stats=dataset_stats,\\\\n        ),\\\\n    ]\\\\n    output_steps = [\\\\n        UnnormalizerProcessorStep(\\\\n            features=config.output_features, norm_map=config.normalization_mapping, stats=dataset_stats\\\\n        ),\\\\n        DeviceProcessorStep(device=\\\\\\\"cpu\\\\\\\"),\\\\n    ]\\\\n    return (\\\\n        PolicyProcessorPipeline[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]](\\\\n            steps=input_steps,\\\\n            name=POLICY_PREPROCESSOR_DEFAULT_NAME,\\\\n        ),\\\\n        PolicyProcessorPipeline[PolicyAction, PolicyAction](\\\\n            steps=output_steps,\\\\n            name=POLICY_POSTPROCESSOR_DEFAULT_NAME,\\\\n            to_transition=policy_action_to_transition,\\\\n            to_output=transition_to_policy_action,\\\\n        ),\\\\n    )\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n@ProcessorStepRegistry.register(name=\\\\\\\"smolvla_new_line_processor\\\\\\\")\\\\nclass SmolVLANewLineProcessor(ComplementaryDataProcessorStep):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    A processor step that ensures the 'task' description ends with a newline character.\\\\n\\\\n    This step is necessary for certain tokenizers (e.g., PaliGemma) that expect a\\\\n    newline at the end of the prompt. It handles both single string tasks and lists\\\\n    of string tasks.\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def complementary_data(self, complementary_data):\\\\n        if \\\\\\\"task\\\\\\\" not in complementary_data:\\\\n            return complementary_data\\\\n\\\\n        task = complementary_data[\\\\\\\"task\\\\\\\"]\\\\n        if task is None:\\\\n            return complementary_data\\\\n\\\\n        new_complementary_data = dict(complementary_data)\\\\n\\\\n        # Handle both string and list of strings\\\\n        if isinstance(task, str):\\\\n            # Single string: add newline if not present\\\\n            if not task.endswith(\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\"):\\\\n                new_complementary_data[\\\\\\\"task\\\\\\\"] = f\\\\\\\"{task}\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\n        elif isinstance(task, list) and all(isinstance(t, str) for t in task):\\\\n            # List of strings: add newline to each if not present\\\\n            new_complementary_data[\\\\\\\"task\\\\\\\"] = [t if t.endswith(\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\") else f\\\\\\\"{t}\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\" for t in task]\\\\n        # If task is neither string nor list of strings, leave unchanged\\\\n\\\\n        return new_complementary_data\\\\n\\\\n    def transform_features(\\\\n        self, features: dict[PipelineFeatureType, dict[str, PolicyFeature]]\\\\n    ) -> dict[PipelineFeatureType, dict[str, PolicyFeature]]:\\\\n        return features\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.74}}\"}"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"87fbfff0-f43b-4166-9bc4-76c8e6a40878","uuid":"26e77f85-c34e-48a5-a88c-9a7ef9f73b28","timestamp":"2026-05-15T18:31:07.565Z","tool_use_result":{"content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"#!/usr/bin/env python\\\\n\\\\n# Copyright 2025 HuggingFace Inc. team. Renaming features to match pretrained configurations.\\\\n    2.  Normalizing input and output features based on dataset statistics.\\\\n    3.  Adding a batch dimension.\\\\n    4.  Ensuring the language task description ends with a newline character.\\\\n    5.  Tokenizing the language task description.\\\\n    6.  Moving all data to the specified device.\\\\n\\\\n    The post-processing pipeline handles the model's output by:\\\\n    1.  Moving data to the CPU.\\\\n    2.  Unnormalizing the output actions to their original scale.\\\\n\\\\n    Args:\\\\n        config: The configuration object for the SmolVLA policy.\\\\n        dataset_stats: A dictionary of statistics for normalization.\\\\n\\\\n    Returns:\\\\n        A tuple containing the configured pre-processor and post-processor pipelines.\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    input_steps = [\\\\n        RenameObservationsProcessorStep(rename_map={}),  # To mimic the same processor as pretrained one\\\\n        AddBatchDimensionProcessorStep(),\\\\n        SmolVLANewLineProcessor(),\\\\n        TokenizerProcessorStep(\\\\n            tokenizer_name=config.vlm_model_name,\\\\n            padding=config.pad_language_to,\\\\n            padding_side=\\\\\\\"right\\\\\\\",\\\\n            max_length=config.tokenizer_max_length,\\\\n        ),\\\\n        DeviceProcessorStep(device=config.device),\\\\n        NormalizerProcessorStep(\\\\n            features={**config.input_features, **config.output_features},\\\\n            norm_map=config.normalization_mapping,\\\\n            stats=dataset_stats,\\\\n        ),\\\\n    ]\\\\n    output_steps = [\\\\n        UnnormalizerProcessorStep(\\\\n            features=config.output_features, norm_map=config.normalization_mapping, stats=dataset_stats\\\\n        ),\\\\n        DeviceProcessorStep(device=\\\\\\\"cpu\\\\\\\"),\\\\n    ]\\\\n    return (\\\\n        PolicyProcessorPipeline[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]](\\\\n            steps=input_steps,\\\\n            name=POLICY_PREPROCESSOR_DEFAULT_NAME,\\\\n        ),\\\\n        PolicyProcessorPipeline[PolicyAction, PolicyAction](\\\\n            steps=output_steps,\\\\n            name=POLICY_POSTPROCESSOR_DEFAULT_NAME,\\\\n            to_transition=policy_action_to_transition,\\\\n            to_output=transition_to_policy_action,\\\\n        ),\\\\n    )\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n@ProcessorStepRegistry.register(name=\\\\\\\"smolvla_new_line_processor\\\\\\\")\\\\nclass SmolVLANewLineProcessor(ComplementaryDataProcessorStep):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    A processor step that ensures the 'task' description ends with a newline character.\\\\n\\\\n    This step is necessary for certain tokenizers (e.g., PaliGemma) that expect a\\\\n    newline at the end of the prompt. It handles both single string tasks and lists\\\\n    of string tasks.\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def complementary_data(self, complementary_data):\\\\n        if \\\\\\\"task\\\\\\\" not in complementary_data:\\\\n            return complementary_data\\\\n\\\\n        task = complementary_data[\\\\\\\"task\\\\\\\"]\\\\n        if task is None:\\\\n            return complementary_data\\\\n\\\\n        new_complementary_data = dict(complementary_data)\\\\n\\\\n        # Handle both string and list of strings\\\\n        if isinstance(task, str):\\\\n            # Single string: add newline if not present\\\\n            if not task.endswith(\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\"):\\\\n                new_complementary_data[\\\\\\\"task\\\\\\\"] = f\\\\\\\"{task}\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\n        elif isinstance(task, list) and all(isinstance(t, str) for t in task):\\\\n            # List of strings: add newline to each if not present\\\\n            new_complementary_data[\\\\\\\"task\\\\\\\"] = [t if t.endswith(\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\") else f\\\\\\\"{t}\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\" for t in task]\\\\n        # If task is neither string nor list of strings, leave unchanged\\\\n\\\\n        return new_complementary_data\\\\n\\\\n    def transform_features(\\\\n        self, features: dict[PipelineFeatureType, dict[str, PolicyFeature]]\\\\n    ) -> dict[PipelineFeatureType, dict[str, PolicyFeature]]:\\\\n        return features\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.74}}\"}","structuredContent":{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"#!/usr/bin/env python\\n\\n# Copyright 2025 HuggingFace Inc. team. All rights reserved.\\n#\\n# Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \\\"License\\\");\\n# you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\\n# You may obtain a copy of the License at\\n#\\n#     http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\\n#\\n# Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\\n# distributed under the License is distributed on an \\\"AS IS\\\" BASIS,\\n# WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\\n# See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\\n# limitations under the License.\\n\\nfrom typing import Any\\n\\nimport torch\\n\\nfrom lerobot.configs.types import PipelineFeatureType, PolicyFeature\\nfrom lerobot.policies.smolvla.configuration_smolvla import SmolVLAConfig\\nfrom lerobot.processor import (\\n    AddBatchDimensionProcessorStep,\\n    ComplementaryDataProcessorStep,\\n    DeviceProcessorStep,\\n    NormalizerProcessorStep,\\n    PolicyAction,\\n    PolicyProcessorPipeline,\\n    ProcessorStepRegistry,\\n    RenameObservationsProcessorStep,\\n    TokenizerProcessorStep,\\n    UnnormalizerProcessorStep,\\n)\\nfrom lerobot.processor.converters import policy_action_to_transition, transition_to_policy_action\\nfrom lerobot.utils.constants import POLICY_POSTPROCESSOR_DEFAULT_NAME, POLICY_PREPROCESSOR_DEFAULT_NAME\\n\\n\\ndef make_smolvla_pre_post_processors(\\n    config: SmolVLAConfig,\\n    dataset_stats: dict[str, dict[str, torch.Tensor]] | None = None,\\n) -> tuple[\\n    PolicyProcessorPipeline[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]],\\n    PolicyProcessorPipeline[PolicyAction, PolicyAction],\\n]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Constructs pre-processor and post-processor pipelines for the SmolVLA policy.\\n\\n    The pre-processing pipeline prepares input data for the model by:\\n    1.  Renaming features to match pretrained configurations.\\n    2.  Normalizing input and output features based on dataset statistics.\\n    3.  Adding a batch dimension.\\n    4.  Ensuring the language task description ends with a newline character.\\n    5.  Tokenizing the language task description.\\n    6.  Moving all data to the specified device.\\n\\n    The post-processing pipeline handles the model's output by:\\n    1.  Moving data to the CPU.\\n    2.  Unnormalizing the output actions to their original scale.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        config: The configuration object for the SmolVLA policy.\\n        dataset_stats: A dictionary of statistics for normalization.\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        A tuple containing the configured pre-processor and post-processor pipelines.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    input_steps = [\\n        RenameObservationsProcessorStep(rename_map={}),  # To mimic the same processor as pretrained one\\n        AddBatchDimensionProcessorStep(),\\n        SmolVLANewLineProcessor(),\\n        TokenizerProcessorStep(\\n            tokenizer_name=config.vlm_model_name,\\n            padding=config.pad_language_to,\\n            padding_side=\\\"right\\\",\\n            max_length=config.tokenizer_max_length,\\n        ),\\n        DeviceProcessorStep(device=config.device),\\n        NormalizerProcessorStep(\\n            features={**config.input_features, **config.output_features},\\n            norm_map=config.normalization_mapping,\\n            stats=dataset_stats,\\n        ),\\n    ]\\n    output_steps = [\\n        UnnormalizerProcessorStep(\\n            features=config.output_features, norm_map=config.normalization_mapping, stats=dataset_stats\\n        ),\\n        DeviceProcessorStep(device=\\\"cpu\\\"),\\n    ]\\n    return (\\n        PolicyProcessorPipeline[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]](\\n            steps=input_steps,\\n            name=POLICY_PREPROCESSOR_DEFAULT_NAME,\\n        ),\\n        PolicyProcessorPipeline[PolicyAction, PolicyAction](\\n            steps=output_steps,\\n            name=POLICY_POSTPROCESSOR_DEFAULT_NAME,\\n            to_transition=policy_action_to_transition,\\n            to_output=transition_to_policy_action,\\n        ),\\n    )\\n\\n\\n@ProcessorStepRegistry.register(name=\\\"smolvla_new_line_processor\\\")\\nclass SmolVLANewLineProcessor(ComplementaryDataProcessorStep):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    A processor step that ensures the 'task' description ends with a newline character.\\n\\n    This step is necessary for certain tokenizers (e.g., PaliGemma) that expect a\\n    newline at the end of the prompt. It handles both single string tasks and lists\\n    of string tasks.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def complementary_data(self, complementary_data):\\n        if \\\"task\\\" not in complementary_data:\\n            return complementary_data\\n\\n        task = complementary_data[\\\"task\\\"]\\n        if task is None:\\n            return complementary_data\\n\\n        new_complementary_data = dict(complementary_data)\\n\\n        # Handle both string and list of strings\\n        if isinstance(task, str):\\n            # Single string: add newline if not present\\n            if not task.endswith(\\\"\\\\n\\\"):\\n                new_complementary_data[\\\"task\\\"] = f\\\"{task}\\\\n\\\"\\n        elif isinstance(task, list) and all(isinstance(t, str) for t in task):\\n            # List of strings: add newline to each if not present\\n            new_complementary_data[\\\"task\\\"] = [t if t.endswith(\\\"\\\\n\\\") else f\\\"{t}\\\\n\\\" for t in task]\\n        # If task is neither string nor list of strings, leave unchanged\\n\\n        return new_complementary_data\\n\\n    def transform_features(\\n        self, features: dict[PipelineFeatureType, dict[str, PolicyFeature]]\\n    ) -> dict[PipelineFeatureType, dict[str, PolicyFeature]]:\\n        return features\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.74}}"}}}
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01JRbbWEgrgwwRyKsaKTyKsJ","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"# Copyright 2025 The HuggingFace Inc. team. All rights reserved.\\\\n#\\\\n# Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \\\\\\\"License\\\\\\\");\\\\n# you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\\\\n# You may obtain a copy of the License at\\\\n#\\\\n#     http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\\\\n#\\\\n# Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\\\\n# distributed under the License is distributed on an \\\\\\\"AS IS\\\\\\\" BASIS,\\\\n# WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\\\\n# See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\\\\n# limitations under the License.\\\\n\\\\nimport copy\\\\n\\\\nimport torch\\\\nfrom torch import nn\\\\nfrom transformers import (\\\\n    AutoConfig,\\\\n    AutoModel,\\\\n    AutoModelForImageTextToText,\\\\n    AutoProcessor,\\\\n    SmolVLMForConditionalGeneration,\\\\n)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef apply_rope(x, positions, max_wavelength=10_000):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    Applies RoPE positions [B, L] to x [B, L, H, D].\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    d_half = x.shape[-1] // 2\\\\n    device = x.device\\\\n    dtype = x.dtype\\\\n    x = x.to(torch.float32)\\\\n\\\\n    freq_exponents = (2.0 / x.shape[-1]) * torch.arange(d_half, dtype=torch.float32, device=device)\\\\n    timescale = max_wavelength**freq_exponents\\\\n    radians = positions[..., None].to(torch.float32) / timescale[None, None, :].to(torch.float32)\\\\n\\\\n    radians = radians[..., None, :]\\\\n\\\\n    sin = torch.sin(radians)  # .to(dtype=dtype)\\\\n    cos = torch.cos(radians)  # .to(dtype=dtype)\\\\n\\\\n    x1, x2 = x.split(d_half, dim=-1)\\\\n    res = torch.empty_like(x)\\\\n    res[..., :d_half] = x1 * cos - x2 * sin\\\\n    res[..., d_half:] = x2 * cos + x1 * sin\\\\n\\\\n    return res.to(dtype)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef get_intermediate_size(hidden_dim, ffn_dim_multiplier=4, multiple_of=256):\\\\n    hidden_dim = int(2 * hidden_dim / 3)\\\\n    hidden_dim = int(ffn_dim_multiplier * hidden_dim)\\\\n    hidden_dim = multiple_of * ((hidden_dim + multiple_of - 1) // multiple_of)\\\\n    return hidden_dim\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass SmolVLMWithExpertModel(nn.Module):\\\\n    def __init__(\\\\n        self,\\\\n        model_id: str = \\\\\\\"HuggingFaceTB/SmolVLM2-500M-Video-Instruct\\\\\\\",\\\\n        load_vlm_weights: bool = True,\\\\n        train_expert_only: bool = True,\\\\n        freeze_vision_encoder: bool = False,\\\\n        attention_mode: str = \\\\\\\"self_attn\\\\\\\",\\\\n        num_expert_layers: int = -1,\\\\n        num_vlm_layers: int = -1,\\\\n        self_attn_every_n_layers: int = -1,\\\\n        expert_width_multiplier: float = 0.5,\\\\n        device: str = \\\\\\\"auto\\\\\\\",\\\\n    ):\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\n        if load_vlm_weights:\\\\n            print(f\\\\\\\"Loading  {model_id} weights ...\\\\\\\")\\\\n            self.vlm = AutoModelForImageTextToText.from_pretrained(\\\\n                model_id,\\\\n                torch_dtype=\\\\\\\"bfloat16\\\\\\\",\\\\n                low_cpu_mem_usage=True,\\\\n            )\\\\n {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01RMCVFAgBSxVkvd6ETwtv5w","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"I see the problem. The existing scripts are missing:\n1. `source /etc/network_turbo`\n2. `REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE` and `SSL_CERT_FILE` environment variables\n3. `HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET=1`\n\nThese are needed for the HuggingFace downloads to work through AutoDL's proxy.\n\nNow, the existing scripts use `lerobot-eval` directly with `--policy.path=lerobot/smolvla_libero` and `--policy.path=lerobot/smolvla_libero_plus`. \n\nThese are HuggingFace repo IDs, so when lerobot loads them, it downloads the policy config and weights from HF, plus the base VLM model (SmolVLM2-500M-Video-Instruct) that the policy config references. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_012Pi5SwE3G2oX4t9jreJaZe","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01CsF6bCb9iQsAYL49oNn5Mr","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"westb-13953","account":"root","command":"source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && python3 << 'PYEOF'\nimport re\n\nenv_block = \"\"\"import os\nos.environ.setdefault('HF_HOME', '/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache')\nos.environ.setdefault('REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE', '/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt')\nos.environ.setdefault('SSL_CERT_FILE', '/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt')\nos.environ.setdefault('HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET', '1')\nos.environ.setdefault('MUJOCO_GL', 'egl')\n\"\"\"\n\nfor fpath in ['/root/acib/scripts/eval_baseline.py', '/root/acib/scripts/train_acib.py']:\n    with open(fpath, 'r') as f:\n        content = f.read()\n    if 'REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE' in content:\n        print(f'{fpath}: already patched')\n        continue\n    # Insert after docstring or shebang\n    lines = content.split('\\n')\n    insert_idx = 0\n    in_docstring = False\n    for i, line in enumerate(lines):\n        if '\"\"\"' in line:\n            if in_docstring:\n                insert_idx = i + 1\n                in_docstring = False\n                break\n            else:\n                in_docstring = True\n                continue\n        if not in_docstring and i < 3 and (line.startswith('#!') or line.strip() == ''):\n            insert_idx = i + 1\n    lines.insert(insert_idx, env_block)\n    with open(fpath, 'w') as f:\n        f.write('\\n'.join(lines))\n    print(f'{fpath}: patched at line {insert_idx}')\nPYEOF"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":657,"cache_read_input_tokens":100588,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":657},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"87fbfff0-f43b-4166-9bc4-76c8e6a40878","uuid":"4c59b088-c11c-4c91-8a5f-40211750119a"}
